        Idea:
Build an agentic test harness for the timeline agent edge function (reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/). The harness should:

1. Work against REAL data — real Supabase account, real timelines, real task creation, real generation pipeline. No mocks.

2. Core loop: set up a real timeline with real assets → send user messages to the real agent endpoint → wait for all side effects to complete (tasks, generations, shots, asset registry updates) → inspect all affected tables (tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines config + asset_registry, users/credits) → evaluate whether the agent did what was asked → report results.

3. An agentic outer loop where Claude generates contextually-relevant test tasks by looking at the actual timeline state (what clips exist, what tracks, what assets), runs them, scores results, identifies failure patterns, proposes code fixes, and re-runs.

4. Task categories to cover:
   - Pure timeline edits (move, trim, delete, duplicate, add-text, set-property)
   - Generation requests (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, image-to-video)
   - Compound operations (generate + place on timeline)
   - Error handling (bad clip refs, ambiguous instructions, version conflicts)

5. The harness needs to handle async completion — tasks go Queued → In Progress → Complete, generations get created by DB triggers, assets get registered. Need to poll/wait for the full cascade.

6. Evaluation should cover: state correctness (did the right things change), no collateral damage (did anything unexpected change), efficiency (loop iterations, LLM calls), error recovery quality.

Key files:
- Edge function: reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/ (index.ts, loop.ts, tools/, llm/, prompts.ts, db.ts)
- Create task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/
- Complete task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete_task/
- DB tables: tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines (config + asset_registry)
- Timeline tools: tools/timeline.ts (view, move, trim, delete, set-property, add-text, duplicate, find-issues)
- Generation tools: tools/create-task.ts (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, scene-transfer, image-to-video)
- Shot management: tools/clips.ts (resolveClipGenerationIds, findShotForGenerations, createShotWithGenerations)

row;\n+    return accumulator;\n+  }, {});\n+}\n+\n+function asTaskIdArray(value: unknown): string[] {\n+  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {\n+    return [];\n+  }\n+\n+  return value.flatMap((item) => typeof item === \"string\" && item.trim() ? [item.trim()] : []);\n+}\n+\n+function getGenerationLinkedTaskIds(row: { tasks: Json | null; params: Json | null }): string[] {\n+  const taskIds = new Set(asTaskIdArray(row.tasks));\n+  if (isRecord(row.params) && typeof row.params.source_task_id === \"string\" && row.params.source_task_id.trim()) {\n+    taskIds.add(row.params.source_task_id.trim());\n+  }\n+\n+  return Array.from(taskIds).sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));\n+}\n+\n+function deepDiff(before: unknown, after: unknown, path = \"\"): ValueChange[] {\n+  if (Object.is(before, after)) {\n+    return [];\n+  }\n+\n+  const beforeIsRecord = isRecord(before);\n+  const afterIsRecord = isRecord(after);\n+  if (beforeIsRecord && afterIsRecord) {\n+    const keys = Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(before), ...Object.keys(after)])).sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));\n+    return keys.flatMap((key) => deepDiff(before[key], after[key], path ? `${path}.${key}` : key));\n+  }\n+\n+  if (Array.isArray(before) && Array.isArray(after)) {\n+    const length = Math.max(before.length, after.length);\n+    const changes: ValueChange[] = [];\n+    for (let index = 0; index < length; index += 1) {\n+      changes.push(...deepDiff(before[index], after[index], `${path}[${index}]`));\n+    }\n+    return changes;\n+  }\n+\n+  return [{ path: path || \"<root>\", before, after }];\n+}\n+\n+function diffRows<Row>(\n+  beforeRows: RowMap<Row>,\n+  afterRows: RowMap<Row>,\n+  createModifiedRow: (before: Row, after: Row) => ModifiedRow<Row> | TimelineModifiedRow = (before, after) => ({\n+    before,\n+    after,\n+    changes: deepDiff(before, after),\n+  }),\n+): TableDiff<Row> {\n+  const added: RowMap<Row> = {};\n+  const removed: RowMap<Row> = {};\n+  const modified: Record<string, ModifiedRow<Row> | TimelineModifiedRow> = {};\n+  const ids = Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(beforeRows), ...Object.keys(afterRows)])).sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));\n+\n+  for (const id of ids) {\n+    const before = beforeRows[id];\n+    const after = afterRows[id];\n+    if (!before && after) {\n+      added[id] = after;\n+      continue;\n+    }\n+    if (before && !after) {\n+      removed[id] = before;\n+      continue;\n+    }\n+    if (before && after) {\n+      const rowDiff = createModifiedRow(before, after);\n+      if (rowDiff.changes.length > 0 || (\"clip_changes\" in rowDiff && Object.keys(rowDiff.clip_changes.added).length + Object.keys(rowDiff.clip_changes.removed).length + Object.keys(rowDiff.clip_changes.modified).length > 0)) {\n+        modified[id] = rowDiff;\n+      }\n+    }\n+  }\n+\n+  return { added, removed, modified };\n+}\n+\n+function createTimelineModifiedRow(before: TimelineSnapshotRow, after: TimelineSnapshotRow): TimelineModifiedRow {\n+  const beforeWithoutClips = {\n+    ...before,\n+    config: {\n+      ...before.config,\n+      clips: [],\n+    },\n+  };\n+  const afterWithoutClips = {\n+    ...after,\n+    config: {\n+      ...after.config,\n+      clips: [],\n+    },\n+  };\n+\n+  return {\n+    before,\n+    after,\n+    changes: deepDiff(beforeWithoutClips, afterWithoutClips),\n+    clip_changes: diffRows(\n+      indexById(before.config.clips),\n+      indexById(after.config.clips),\n+      (previousClip, nextClip) => ({\n+        before: previousClip,\n+        after: nextClip,\n+        changes: deepDiff(previousClip, nextClip),\n+      }),\n+    ),\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+async function selectRows<Row>(query: PromiseLike<{ data: Row[] | null; error: { message: string } | null }>, label: string): Promise<Row[]> {\n+  const { data, error } = await query;\n+  if (error) {\n+    throw new Error(`Failed to load ${label}: ${error.message}`);\n+  }\n+\n+  return data ?? [];\n+}\n+\n+async function selectSingle<Row>(query: PromiseLike<{ data: Row | null; error: { message: string } | null }>, label: string): Promise<Row | null> {\n+  const { data, error } = await query;\n+  if (error) {\n+    throw new Error(`Failed to load ${label}: ${error.message}`);\n+  }\n+\n+  return data;\n+}\n+\n+export async function snapshotState(\n+  timelineId: string,\n+  projectId: string,\n+  userId: string,\n+): Promise<HarnessSnapshot> {\n+  const supabase = getAdminSupabaseClient();\n+  const [timeline, tasks, generations, shots, sessions, credits, user] = await Promise.all([\n+    selectSingle(\n+      supabase.from(\"timelines\").select(\"id, config, config_version, asset_registry\").eq(\"id\", timelineId).maybeSingle(),\n+      \"timeline\",\n+    ),\n+    selectRows(\n+      supabase.from(\"tasks\").select(\"id, task_type, params, status, output_location, error_message, created_at, generation_created\").eq(\"project_id\", projectId).order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true }),\n+      \"tasks\",\n+    ),\n+    selectRows(\n+      supabase.from(\"generations\").select(\"id, tasks, params, location, thumbnail_url, type, primary_variant_id\").eq(\"project_id\", projectId).order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true }),\n+      \"generations\",\n+    ),\n+    selectRows(\n+      supabase.from(\"shots\").select(\"id, name, position, aspect_ratio, settings\").eq(\"project_id\", projectId).order(\"position\", { ascending: true }),\n+      \"shots\",\n+    ),\n+    selectRows(\n+      supabase.from(\"timeline_agent_sessions\").select(\"id, turns, status, model, summary\").eq(\"timeline_id\", timelineId).order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true }),\n+      \"timeline_agent_sessions\",\n+    ),\n+    selectRows(\n+      supabase.from(\"credits_ledger\").select(\"id, amount, type, task_id, metadata\").eq(\"user_id\", userId).order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true }),\n+      \"credits_ledger\",\n+    ),\n+    selectSingle(\n+      supabase.from(\"users\").select(\"id, email, credits\").eq(\"id\", userId).maybeSingle(),\n+      \"user credits\",\n+    ),\n+  ]);\n+\n+  const generationIds = generations.flatMap((row) => typeof row?.id === \"string\" ? [row.id] : []);\n+  const shotIds = shots.flatMap((row) => typeof row?.id === \"string\" ? [row.id] : []);\n+  const [generationVariants, shotGenerations] = await Promise.all([\n+    generationIds.length > 0\n+      ? selectRows(\n+        supabase.from(\"generation_variants\").select(\"id, generation_id, location, thumbnail_url, is_primary, variant_type, name, params, created_at, project_id\").in(\"generation_id\", generationIds).order(\"created_at\", { ascending: true }),\n+        \"generation_variants\",\n+      )\n+      : Promise.resolve([] as GenerationVariantSnapshotRow[]),\n+    shotIds.length > 0\n+      ? selectRows(\n+        supabase.from(\"shot_generations\").select(\"id, shot_id, generation_id, timeline_frame, metadata, created_at, updated_at\").in(\"shot_id\", shotIds).order(\"updated_at\", { ascending: true }),\n+        \"shot_generations\",\n+      )\n+      : Promise.resolve([] as ShotGenerationSnapshotRow[]),\n+  ]);\n+\n+  return {\n+    captured_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n+    timeline_id: timelineId,\n+    project_id: projectId,\n+    user_id: userId,\n+    user: user && typeof user.id === \"string\" && typeof user.credits === \"number\"\n+      ? { id: user.id, email: typeof user.email === \"string\" ? user.email : null, credits: user.credits }\n+      : null,\n+    timelines: timeline && typeof timeline.id === \"string\"\n+      ? indexById([{\n+        id: timeline.id,\n+        config: (timeline as { config: TimelineConfig }).config,\n+        config_version: typeof (timeline as { config_version?: unknown }).config_version === \"number\"\n+          ? (timeline as { config_version: number }).config_version\n+          : 1,\n+        asset_registry: (timeline as { asset_registry?: Json }).asset_registry ?? { assets: {} },\n+      }])\n+      : {},\n+    tasks: indexById(tasks as TaskSnapshotRow[]),\n+    generations: indexById((generations as Array<Omit<GenerationSnapshotRow, \"linked_task_ids\">>).map((row) => ({\n+      ...row,\n+      linked_task_ids: getGenerationLinkedTaskIds(row),\n+    }))),\n+    generation_variants: indexById(generationVariants as GenerationVariantSnapshotRow[]),\n+    shot_generations: indexById(shotGenerations as ShotGenerationSnapshotRow[]),\n+    shots: indexById(shots as ShotSnapshotRow[]),\n+    timeline_agent_sessions: indexById(sessions as SessionSnapshotRow[]),\n+    credits_ledger: indexById(credits as CreditsLedgerSnapshotRow[]),\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export function diffSnapshots(before: HarnessSnapshot, after: HarnessSnapshot): SnapshotDiff {\n+  return {\n+    user: before.user && after.user\n+      ? (() => {\n+        const changes = deepDiff(before.user, after.user);\n+        return changes.length > 0 ? { before: before.user, after: after.user, changes } : null;\n+      })()\n+      : null,\n+    timelines: diffRows(before.timelines, after.timelines, createTimelineModifiedRow),\n+    tasks: diffRows(before.tasks, after.tasks),\n+    generations: diffRows(before.generations, after.generations),\n+    generation_variants: diffRows(before.generation_variants, after.generation_variants),\n+    shot_generations: diffRows(before.shot_generations, after.shot_generations),\n+    shots: diffRows(before.shots, after.shots),\n+    timeline_agent_sessions: diffRows(before.timeline_agent_sessions, after.timeline_agent_sessions),\n+    credits_ledger: diffRows(before.credits_ledger, after.credits_ledger),\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+function summarizeTable<Row extends { id: string }>(\n+  label: string,\n+  table: TableDiff<Row>,\n+  renderAdded?: (row: Row) => string,\n+  renderModified?: (id: string, row: ModifiedRow<Row> | TimelineModifiedRow) => string,\n+): string[] {\n+  const lines = [\n+    `${label}: +${Object.keys(table.added).length} -${Object.keys(table.removed).length} ~${Object.keys(table.modified).length}`,\n+  ];\n+\n+  for (const row of Object.values(table.added)) {\n+    lines.push(`  + ${renderAdded ? renderAdded(row) : row.id}`);\n+  }\n+  for (const row of Object.values(table.removed)) {\n+    lines.push(`  - ${row.id}`);\n+  }\n+  for (const [id, row] of Object.entries(table.modified)) {\n+    lines.push(`  ~ ${renderModified ? renderModified(id, row) : `${id} (${row.changes.map((change) => change.path).join(\", \")})`}`);\n+  }\n+\n+  return lines;\n+}\n+\n+export function summarizeDiff(diff: SnapshotDiff): string {\n+  const lines: string[] = [];\n+\n+  if (diff.user) {\n+    lines.push(`user credits: ${diff.user.before.credits} -> ${diff.user.after.credits}`);\n+  }\n+\n+  lines.push(\n+    ...summarizeTable(\"timelines\", diff.timelines, (row) => row.id, (id, row) => {\n+      const clipChangeCount = \"clip_changes\" in row\n+        ? Object.keys(row.clip_changes.added).length + Object.keys(row.clip_changes.removed).length + Object.keys(row.clip_changes.modified).length\n+        : 0;\n+      const parts = row.changes.map((change) => change.path);\n+      if (clipChangeCount > 0) parts.push(`clips(${clipChangeCount})`);\n+      return `${id} (${parts.join(\", \") || \"changed\"})`;\n+    }),\n+    ...summarizeTable(\"tasks\", diff.tasks, (row) => `${row.id} [${row.task_type}] ${row.status}`),\n+    ...summarizeTable(\"generations\", diff.generations, (row) => `${row.id} linked_tasks=${row.linked_task_ids.join(\",\") || \"none\"}`),\n+    ...summarizeTable(\"generation_variants\", diff.generation_variants),\n+    ...summarizeTable(\"shot_generations\", diff.shot_generations),\n+    ...summarizeTable(\"shots\", diff.shots, (row) => `${row.id} ${row.name}`),\n+    ...summarizeTable(\"timeline_agent_sessions\", diff.timeline_agent_sessions, (row) => `${row.id} ${row.status}`),\n+    ...summarizeTable(\"credits_ledger\", diff.credits_ledger, (row) => `${row.id} ${row.type} ${row.amount}`),\n+  );\n+\n+  return lines.join(\"\\n\");\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace

        Idea:
Build an agentic test harness for the timeline agent edge function (reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/). The harness should:

1. Work against REAL data — real Supabase account, real timelines, real task creation, real generation pipeline. No mocks.

2. Core loop: set up a real timeline with real assets → send user messages to the real agent endpoint → wait for all side effects to complete (tasks, generations, shots, asset registry updates) → inspect all affected tables (tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines config + asset_registry, users/credits) → evaluate whether the agent did what was asked → report results.

3. An agentic outer loop where Claude generates contextually-relevant test tasks by looking at the actual timeline state (what clips exist, what tracks, what assets), runs them, scores results, identifies failure patterns, proposes code fixes, and re-runs.

4. Task categories to cover:
   - Pure timeline edits (move, trim, delete, duplicate, add-text, set-property)
   - Generation requests (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, image-to-video)
   - Compound operations (generate + place on timeline)
   - Error handling (bad clip refs, ambiguous instructions, version conflicts)

5. The harness needs to handle async completion — tasks go Queued → In Progress → Complete, generations get created by DB triggers, assets get registered. Need to poll/wait for the full cascade.

6. Evaluation should cover: state correctness (did the right things change), no collateral damage (did anything unexpected change), efficiency (loop iterations, LLM calls), error recovery quality.

Key files:
- Edge function: reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/ (index.ts, loop.ts, tools/, llm/, prompts.ts, db.ts)
- Create task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/
- Complete task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete_task/
- DB tables: tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines (config + asset_registry)
- Timeline tools: tools/timeline.ts (view, move, trim, delete, set-property, add-text, duplicate, find-issues)
- Generation tools: tools/create-task.ts (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, scene-transfer, image-to-video)
- Shot management: tools/clips.ts (resolveClipGenerationIds, findShotForGenerations, createShotWithGenerations)

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. [row.task_id] : []));\n+  const pending = taskIds.filter((taskId) => !foundTaskIds.has(taskId));\n+  const seen = ledgerRows.map((row) => `${row.id}[task=${row.task_id ?? \"null\"},type=${row.type},amount=${row.amount}]`);\n+  return `Timed out waiting for credits_ledger rows for task ids ${pending.join(\", \")}. Seen entries: ${seen.join(\", \") || \"none\"}`;\n+}\n+\n+export async function waitForTaskCompletion(\n+  taskIds: string[],\n+  timeoutMs = 120_000,\n+): Promise<TaskSnapshotRow[]> {\n+  const uniqueTaskIds = Array.from(new Set(taskIds));\n+  if (uniqueTaskIds.length === 0) {\n+    return [];\n+  }\n+\n+  const startedAt = Date.now();\n+  while (Date.now() - startedAt <= timeoutMs) {\n+    const rows = await fetchTasks(uniqueTaskIds);\n+    const terminal = rows.length === uniqueTaskIds.length\n+      && rows.every((row) => TASK_TERMINAL_STATUSES.has(row.status));\n+    if (terminal) {\n+      return rows;\n+    }\n+\n+    await delay(POLL_INTERVAL_MS);\n+  }\n+\n+  const rows = await fetchTasks(uniqueTaskIds);\n+  throw new Error(formatTaskTimeout(rows, uniqueTaskIds));\n+}\n+\n+export async function waitForGenerations(\n+  taskIds: string[],\n+  timeoutMs = 120_000,\n+): Promise<GenerationSnapshotRow[]> {\n+  const uniqueTaskIds = Array.from(new Set(taskIds));\n+  if (uniqueTaskIds.length === 0) {\n+    return [];\n+  }\n+\n+  const startedAt = Date.now();\n+  while (Date.now() - startedAt <= timeoutMs) {\n+    const generations = (await Promise.all(uniqueTaskIds.map((taskId) => fetchGenerationsForTask(taskId)))).flat();\n+    const matchedTaskIds = new Set(generations.flatMap((row) => row.linked_task_ids));\n+    if (uniqueTaskIds.every((taskId) => matchedTaskIds.has(taskId))) {\n+      return generations;\n+    }\n+\n+    await delay(POLL_INTERVAL_MS);\n+  }\n+\n+  const generations = (await Promise.all(uniqueTaskIds.map((taskId) => fetchGenerationsForTask(taskId)))).flat();\n+  throw new Error(formatGenerationTimeout(uniqueTaskIds, generations));\n+}\n+\n+export async function waitForCreditsLedger(\n+  taskIds: string[],\n+  timeoutMs = 30_000,\n+): Promise<CreditsLedgerSnapshotRow[]> {\n+  const uniqueTaskIds = Array.from(new Set(taskIds));\n+  if (uniqueTaskIds.length === 0) {\n+    return [];\n+  }\n+\n+  const startedAt = Date.now();\n+  while (Date.now() - startedAt <= timeoutMs) {\n+    const rows = await fetchCreditsLedger(uniqueTaskIds);\n+    const foundTaskIds = new Set(rows.flatMap((row) => row.task_id ? [row.task_id] : []));\n+    if (uniqueTaskIds.every((taskId) => foundTaskIds.has(taskId))) {\n+      return rows;\n+    }\n+\n+    await delay(POLL_INTERVAL_MS);\n+  }\n+\n+  const rows = await fetchCreditsLedger(uniqueTaskIds);\n+  throw new Error(formatCreditsTimeout(uniqueTaskIds, rows));\n+}\n+\n+export function extractNewTaskIds(\n+  beforeSnapshot: HarnessSnapshot,\n+  afterSnapshot: HarnessSnapshot,\n+): string[] {\n+  return Object.keys(afterSnapshot.tasks)\n+    .filter((taskId) => !(taskId in beforeSnapshot.tasks))\n+    .sort((left, right) => left.localeCompare(right));\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"
}

 }\n+  }\n+\n+  return null;\n+}\n+\n+function clipDuration(clip: TimelineClip): number | null {\n+  if (typeof clip.hold === \"number\") {\n+    return clip.hold;\n+  }\n+  if (typeof clip.from === 0 : Math.round((passed / assertions.length) * 100),\n+    details: assertions,\n+  };\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace

        Idea:
Build an agentic test harness for the timeline agent edge function (reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/). The harness should:

1. Work against REAL data — real Supabase account, real timelines, real task creation, real generation pipeline. No mocks.

2. Core loop: set up a real timeline with real assets → send user messages to the real agent endpoint → wait for all side effects to complete (tasks, generations, shots, asset registry updates) → inspect all affected tables (tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines config + asset_registry, users/credits) → evaluate whether the agent did what was asked → report results.

3. An agentic outer loop where Claude generates contextually-relevant test tasks by looking at the actual timeline state (what clips exist, what tracks, what assets), runs them, scores results, identifies failure patterns, proposes code fixes, and re-runs.

4. Task categories to cover:
   - Pure timeline edits (move, trim, delete, duplicate, add-text, set-property)
   - Generation requests (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, image-to-video)
   - Compound operations (generate + place on timeline)
   - Error handling (bad clip refs, ambiguous instructions, version conflicts)

5. The harness needs to handle async completion — tasks go Queued → In Progress → Complete, generations get created by DB triggers, assets get registered. Need to poll/wait for the full cascade.

6. Evaluation should cover: state correctness (did the right things change), no collateral damage (did anything unexpected change), efficiency (loop iterations, LLM calls), error recovery quality.

Key files:
- Edge function: reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/ (index.ts, loop.ts, tools/, llm/, prompts.ts, db.ts)
- Create task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/
- Complete task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete_task/
- DB tables: tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines (config + asset_registry)
- Timeline tools: tools/timeline.ts (view, move, trim, delete, set-property, add-text, duplicate, find-issues)
- Generation tools: tools/create-task.ts (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, scene-transfer, image-to-video)
- Shot management: tools/clips.ts (resolveClipGenerationIds, findShotForGenerations, createShotWithGenerations)

        Idea:
Build an agentic test harness for the timeline agent edge function (reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/). The harness should:

1. Work against REAL data — real Supabase account, real timelines, real task creation, real generation pipeline. No mocks.

2. Core loop: set up a real timeline with real assets → send user messages to the real agent endpoint → wait for all side effects to complete (tasks, generations, shots, asset registry updates) → inspect all affected tables (tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines config + asset_registry, users/credits) → evaluate whether the agent did what was asked → report results.

3. An agentic outer loop where Claude generates contextually-relevant test tasks by looking at the actual timeline state (what clips exist, what tracks, what assets), runs them, scores results, identifies failure patterns, proposes code fixes, and re-runs.

4. Task categories to cover:
   - Pure timeline edits (move, trim, delete, duplicate, add-text, set-property)
   - Generation requests (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, image-to-video)
   - Compound operations (generate + place on timeline)
   - Error handling (bad clip refs, ambiguous instructions, version conflicts)

5. The harness needs to handle async completion — tasks go Queued → In Progress → Complete, generations get created by DB triggers, assets get registered. Need to poll/wait for the full cascade.

6. Evaluation should cover: state correctness (did the right things change), no collateral damage (did anything unexpected change), efficiency (loop iterations, LLM calls), error recovery quality.

Key files:
- Edge function: reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/ (index.ts, loop.ts, tools/, llm/, prompts.ts, db.ts)
- Create task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/
- Complete task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete_task/
- DB tables: tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines (config + asset_registry)
- Timeline tools: tools/timeline.ts (view, move, trim, delete, set-property, add-text, duplicate, find-issues)
- Generation tools: tools/create-task.ts (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, scene-transfer, image-to-video)
- Shot management: tools/clips.ts (resolveClipGenerationIds, findShotForGenerations, createShotWithGenerations)

  "watch_items": [
    "Auto-continue loop: the agent returns `status: \"continue\"` and expects reinvocation with same session_id but NO user_message. Must match useAgentSession.ts:276-288 exactly or tests will snapshot mid-execution.",
    "DB schema correctness: tasks use `task_type` + `params` (NOT `family`/`input`). Generations link via `generations.tasks` JSON field and `params.source_task_id`. Get this wrong and snapshots/diffs will be empty or wrong.",
    "credits_ledger must be included in snapshots and cleanup \u2014 omitting it means billing bugs go undetected and test data leaks.",
    "No compound test category (generate + place on timeline) \u2014 agent has no tool for post-generation clip insertion. Document as blocked, do not silently skip or try to test it.",
    "Edge function test config: verify the correct vitest config file name before adding include patterns. Prior debt item notes plan references to wrong config entry point.",
    "Real money/GPU risk: generation tasks cost real GPU time and credits. The agentic runner MUST respect --max-tasks limit and --dry-run flag.",
    "Cleanup is best-effort: if harness crashes mid-run, orphaned test data remains. This is acceptable but worth noting.",

        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace

        Idea:
Build an agentic test harness for the timeline agent edge function (reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/). The harness should:

1. Work against REAL data — real Supabase account, real timelines, real task creation, real generation pipeline. No mocks.

2. Core loop: set up a real timeline with real assets → send user messages to the real agent endpoint → wait for all side effects to complete (tasks, generations, shots, asset registry updates) → inspect all affected tables (tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines config + asset_registry, users/credits) → evaluate whether the agent did what was asked → report results.

3. An agentic outer loop where Claude generates contextually-relevant test tasks by looking at the actual timeline state (what clips exist, what tracks, what assets), runs them, scores results, identifies failure patterns, proposes code fixes, and re-runs.

4. Task categories to cover:
   - Pure timeline edits (move, trim, delete, duplicate, add-text, set-property)
   - Generation requests (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, image-to-video)
   - Compound operations (generate + place on timeline)
   - Error handling (bad clip refs, ambiguous instructions, version conflicts)

5. The harness needs to handle async completion — tasks go Queued → In Progress → Complete, generations get created by DB triggers, assets get registered. Need to poll/wait for the full cascade.

6. Evaluation should cover: state correctness (did the right things change), no collateral damage (did anything unexpected change), efficiency (loop iterations, LLM calls), error recovery quality.

Key files:
- Edge function: reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/ (index.ts, loop.ts, tools/, llm/, prompts.ts, db.ts)
- Create task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/
- Complete task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete_task/
- DB tables: tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines (config + asset_registry)
- Timeline tools: tools/timeline.ts (view, move, trim, delete, set-property, add-text, duplicate, find-issues)
- Generation tools: tools/create-task.ts (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, scene-transfer, image-to-video)
- Shot management: tools/clips.ts (resolveClipGenerationIds, findShotForGenerations, createShotWithGenerations)

Don't build a full conversation loop; 3 rounds of generate-tests \u2192 run \u2192 analyze is sufficient.\n\n**Testing reality:** Since this harness hits real deployed infrastructure, you can't run it in a normal test loop.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. \"passed\" : \"failed\";\n+\n+    return {\n+      name: testCase.name,\n+      category: testCase.category,\n+      status,\n+      score: evaluation.score,\n+      passed: evaluation.passed,\n+      failed: evaluation.failed,\n+      wall_time_ms: Date.now() - startedAt,\n+      reinvocations: Math.max(0, responses.length - 1),\n+      tasks_created: taskIds.length,\n+      task_ids: taskIds,\n+      assertion_details: evaluation.details,\n+      agent_statuses: responses.map((response) => response.status),\n+      diff_summary: harness.summarizeDiff(diff),\n+    };\n+  } catch (error) {\n+    const message = error instanceof Error ? Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace

        Idea:
Build an agentic test harness for the timeline agent edge function (reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/). The harness should:

1. Work against REAL data — real Supabase account, real timelines, real task creation, real generation pipeline. No mocks.

2. Core loop: set up a real timeline with real assets → send user messages to the real agent endpoint → wait for all side effects to complete (tasks, generations, shots, asset registry updates) → inspect all affected tables (tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines config + asset_registry, users/credits) → evaluate whether the agent did what was asked → report results.

3. An agentic outer loop where Claude generates contextually-relevant test tasks by looking at the actual timeline state (what clips exist, what tracks, what assets), runs them, scores results, identifies failure patterns, proposes code fixes, and re-runs.

4. Task categories to cover:
   - Pure timeline edits (move, trim, delete, duplicate, add-text, set-property)
   - Generation requests (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, image-to-video)
   - Compound operations (generate + place on timeline)
   - Error handling (bad clip refs, ambiguous instructions, version conflicts)

5. The harness needs to handle async completion — tasks go Queued → In Progress → Complete, generations get created by DB triggers, assets get registered. Need to poll/wait for the full cascade.

6. Evaluation should cover: state correctness (did the right things change), no collateral damage (did anything unexpected change), efficiency (loop iterations, LLM calls), error recovery quality.

Key files:
- Edge function: reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/ (index.ts, loop.ts, tools/, llm/, prompts.ts, db.ts)
- Create task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/
- Complete task: reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete_task/
- DB tables: tasks, generations, generation_variants, shot_generations, shots, timelines (config + asset_registry)
- Timeline tools: tools/timeline.ts (view, move, trim, delete, set-property, add-text, duplicate, find-issues)
- Generation tools: tools/create-task.ts (text-to-image, style-transfer, subject-transfer, scene-transfer, image-to-video)
- Shot management: tools/clips.ts (resolveClipGenerationIds, findShotForGenerations, createShotWithGenerations)
